# Graph Report - thingtime  (2026-10-17)

## Corpus Check
- 3834 files · ~9,940,461 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 94 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: (none) 29, .css 15, .entitlements 8)

## Summary
- 57218 nodes · 161859 edges · 1191 communities (1089 shown, 102 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 96% EXTRACTED · 4% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 5897 edges (avg confidence: 0.88)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `441cdf11`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
